Continuous Chain-of-Thought 的训练问题：如何把长文本推理轨迹压缩成少量连续 latent，同时避免现有间接监督方法在训练时需要逐步自回归生成 latent 的高开销。论文进一步指出，当前很多 ContinuousCoT 结论主要来自短、结构化推理轨迹，在更接近真实模型输出的长 CoT 上并不稳。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在两个层面。第一，它把 latent reasoning 的训练目标重新拉回到语言模型熟悉的 token prediction，而不是直接回归连续向量或预测 latent 分布，这对预训练目标和后训练目标的接口设计有参考意义。第二，它给出一个较清楚的负结果：短结构化 CoT 上有效的连续思维方法，在真实、冗长、半自然语言推理轨迹上会明显退化。这提示 latent reasoning 不能只看压缩率和短 benchmark 表现，还要看训练分布、推理时误差累积、以及 latent 是否真的承载可迁移的推理状态。","technical_approach":"论文提出 C-MTP，一种直接监督的 ContinuousCoT 训练方法。已有间接监督方法，如 Coconut 类方法，会让模型逐步生成 latent，并把这些 latent 再喂回模型，训练过程接近推理过程，但需要顺序前向，训练慢。CoLaR 类直接监督方法把 CoT token embedding 聚合后建模成高斯 latent 分布，训练可以并行，但要在连续空间拟合分布，优化较难。C-MTP 的做法更简单：把每个推理步骤中的 token embedding 聚合为一个连续 latent 的目标表示，同时不直接要求模型回归这个向量，而是用 Multi-Token Prediction 的方式，让模型在 latent 位置预测被压缩片段中的多个 token。这样做有两个好处：训练仍然是 token-level likelihood，和预训练目标更接近；训练时可以用 teacher forcing 并行处理完整 CoT，不需要像间接监督那样逐步展开 latent。代价也很明确：直接监督依赖原始 CoT 的分段和长度，压缩能力受 teacher trace 结构限制；推理时模型生成自己的 latent，训练时却看到 teacher-forced 上下文，会带来误差累积。","experiments":"实验把 ContinuousCoT 分成两个训练和评测 regime。第一类是短、结构化推理轨迹，通常少于 100 token，包括 ProsQA、ProntoQA 这类图搜索或符号推理任务。在这个设置下，C-MTP 明显优于 CoLaR 这类直接监督 baseline，并能达到或超过更慢的间接监督方法；论文还强调它训练更快、样本效率更好，对分布外问题泛化更稳。第二类是更长、更接近自然语言的半自然 CoT。这里间接监督方法反而占优，因为它的递归训练目标更适合把长轨迹压缩成少数 latent，而 C-MTP 受原始 trace 长度和 teacher forcing mismatch 影响更大。第三类是来自强 teacher 的现实 CoT。论文发现直接监督和间接监督都明显落后于普通 CoT-SFT，即保留文本推理轨迹做监督微调。这是文中最有信息量的结果：ContinuousCoT 在结构化任务上的优势，不能直接外推到真实推理轨迹。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25 到 35 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中对 direct supervision 和 indirect supervision 的定义，这决定了论文的主要判断框架。Methodology 中 C-MTP 的目标函数值得细看，尤其是它如何把 latent 训练改写为 multi-token prediction，而不是 continuous regression。实验部分建议优先看三组设置的对比：短结构化 trace、冗长半自然 trace、强 teacher 现实 trace。最值得深挖的是失败分析：为什么 direct supervision 在短 trace 上更高效，但在长 trace 上输给递归压缩；为什么两类 ContinuousCoT 到真实 CoT 后都落后于 CoT-SFT。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies how to train Continuous Chain-of-Thought models that replace long textual reasoning traces with a small number of dense latent states. The central issue is not only efficiency. It also asks whether results obtained on short, structured traces survive when the traces become verbose and closer to real model-generated reasoning.","importance":"The main value for pretraining and foundation-model research is the connection between latent reasoning and the language-modeling objective. C-MTP avoids treating latent reasoning as a separate continuous regression problem and instead supervises it through multi-token prediction, which is closer to the objective used during pretraining. The paper is also useful because it reports a clear boundary: ContinuousCoT methods look strong on short structured traces, but their advantage weakens or disappears on realistic long-form reasoning traces. This matters for anyone considering latent reasoning as a compute-saving replacement for textual CoT.","technical_approach":"$12","experiments":"The experiments separate three regimes. On short structured reasoning traces, typically under 100 tokens, C-MTP outperforms previous direct-supervision methods and is competitive with, or better than, slower indirect-supervision methods. The paper reports better training efficiency, better sample efficiency, and stronger out-of-distribution generalization in this regime. On longer semi-natural traces, indirect supervision becomes stronger because its recurrent objective is better suited to compressing long reasoning trajectories into a small number of latent states. On realistic traces generated by a strong teacher, both direct and indirect ContinuousCoT methods fall well short of standard CoT supervised fine-tuning. This is the key empirical message: structured reasoning benchmarks can overstate the practical utility of continuous latent reasoning.","reading_guide":"Skim with attention to the regime split; 25 to 35 minutes is enough for the main ideas. Read the introduction for the distinction between direct and indirect supervision. Then read the C-MTP objective in the methodology section, focusing on how multi-token prediction replaces latent-space distribution matching. In the experiments, prioritize the tables or figures that compare short structured traces, verbose semi-natural traces, and realistic teacher-generated CoT. The most useful part is the failure analysis around compression, teacher forcing, and error accumulation.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"长CoT轨迹下连续推理性能暴跌的发现很有价值，直接看§4长轨迹实验和对应图表即可，方法部分可略读。","en":"The sharp performance drop of continuous CoT on long reasoning traces is a valuable finding; focus on §4 long-trace experiments and skip the method section."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Varun Yerram","He He","Eunsol Choi"],"notable_authors":["He He","Eunsol Choi"],"tags":["continuous chain of thought","latent reasoning","reasoning training","direct supervision"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16972","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.16972","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.17427","title":"Abliteration Is Not a Scalpel: Off-Target Effects of Refusal Removal on Decision Disposition Across Model Families","abstract":"Abliteration - deleting a model's refusal direction from its weights - is the standard recipe behind popular \"uncensored\" open-weight models. We show the surgery is not clean. As a disposition probe we use 21,600 decisions under uncertainty - weekly up/down calls on 60 Warsaw Stock Exchange equities over 18 weeks, replayed through a frozen pipeline so the decision-layer model is the only variable. The task elicits no refusals at all, so any between-arm delta is pure side effect. provenan","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-19T22:27:00Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2607_17427.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL","q-fin.CP"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作检验一个被默认接受的前提：abliteration 只是去掉 refusal，不会改动别的行为倾向。作者关心的不是安全对齐本身，而是更细的“decision disposition”——在没有任何拒答触发的任务里，模型的风险偏好、乐观程度、措辞密度、置信表达会不会被这种权重手术顺带改掉。这个问题抓得很准，因为很多开源“uncensored”模型就是这样做出来的，但社区通常只看 jailbreak 成功率和少量能力 benchmark，很少测远离 refusal 场景的行为漂移。","importance":"对基座研究的启发主要有两层。第一，它提醒人们：低秩方向编辑、权重正交化这类看起来局部的干预，并不一定是局部生效。即便目标行为完全没有被触发，模型的先验倾向也可能整体偏移。第二，它把“能力不变”与“行为分布不变”明确区分开了。对预训练和后训练接口来说，这很重要：很多 recipe 只盯住 benchmark 均值，却忽略了输出分布、决策风格、风险偏好这些更接近 agent deployment 的量。量级上，这不是提出新训练范式的论文，但它对 model editing、alignment surgery、post-train weight intervention 的评估方式有直接修正作用。","technical_approach":"方法设计的关键是把 refusal 影响彻底隔离掉。作者构造了一个冻结的多代理上游流程：给定固定的分析师材料和辩论记录，让决策层模型对 60 只华沙证券交易所股票做 18 周的涨跌判断，每个单元采样 5 次，总计 21,600 个决策。因为这个任务天然不会触发拒答，base 模型在 10,800 次决策里一次都没拒绝，所以 abliteration 前后的差异可以解释为纯副作用，而不是“拒答被移除后的直接结果”。\n\n实验上强调 provenance control。作者固定官方 BF16 checkpoint、统一的 abliteration recipe、冻结 prompt 和上游 pipeline，避免把量化器、来源不一致、prompt 漂移混进结论里。指标上，预注册的主指标是 optimism rate，也就是在相同证据下选择“上涨”的比例；另外还看 verbalized confidence、自我论证长度、不确定性词汇使用等。作者还加入 capability covariate control，试图排除“只是 instruction following 变差了，所以输出风格变了”这种解释。\n\n从机制上看，这篇论文没有像表示工程论文那样去追踪具体层和具体方向的几何结构，而是把重点放在行为读出：如果 refusal direction 真是一个干净、可分离的子空间，那么在完全无 refusal 的任务上，输出分布应当近似不变；现在观察到跨家族一致的 optimism shift 和 uncertainty vocabulary thinning，说明被正交化掉的方向很可能与更广义的谨慎、犹豫、保守表达共享表示成分，至少在现有 chat model 的参数化里并不独立。","experiments":"最重要的结果有四个。第一，作者在两大家族上都观察到方向一致的 optimism shift：abliterated 模型在相同输入下更倾向于给出“上涨”判断。摘要里没有给出具体百分点，全文强调这是预注册主结论，并且跨 family 复现。第二，探索性结果显示，自我论证更冗长，同时不确定性相关词汇变少，说明变化不只是二元决策边界移动，还包括解释层的语气和词汇分布变化。第三，expressed confidence 没有跨家族稳定泛化，甚至出现方向相反，这一点很重要，因为它说明副作用不是简单的“整体更自信”，而是更细粒度的 disposition 重排。第四，作者复查了先前 pilot 中“doubt 被切除”的现象，发现那是 contaminated pair——量化器不匹配——造成的假象；清理 provenance 后结论翻转。这一节对做模型对比实验的人很有警示意义。\n\n从实验设计强度看，21,600 个决策、5 次重复采样、零 generation failure、冻结上游，只让 decision-layer model 成为唯一变量，这种控制在行为研究里算比较扎实。局限也很明显：任务是金融语境下的二元方向判断，虽然作者强调它只是 disposition probe，但外推到更广泛 agent setting 仍需更多任务验证。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和贡献列表，抓住作者到底在反驳什么：不是反驳 refusal removal 有效，而是反驳“它像手术刀一样干净”。然后重点看实验设计部分，尤其是 frozen multi-agent pipeline、preregistration、provenance control 这几处；这决定了结论是否可信。结果部分建议优先看 optimism rate 的主表和按 family 分解的图，再看 uncertainty vocabulary 与 verbosity 的分析。最后看 pilot-correction 那一节，价值不在具体金融任务，而在它展示了 quantizer mismatch 如何制造行为差异假象。预计 20–30 分钟可以抓住主要信息；如果你做 model editing 或 post-train weight surgery，值得把附录里的 endpoint 定义和控制变量也过一遍。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tests a widely assumed claim behind abliteration: removing the refusal direction should only suppress refusals and leave everything else intact. The authors ask a sharper question—whether a weight-space refusal edit also shifts a model’s decision disposition in tasks where refusals never occur at all. They operationalize disposition through choices under uncertainty, expressed confidence, verbosity of self-justification, and uncertainty language. That framing matters because most public “uncensored” checkpoints are produced via exactly this recipe, yet evaluation usually stops at jailbreak behavior and coarse capability benchmarks.","importance":"For foundation-model research, the contribution is less about a new training method and more about correcting how intervention effects are measured. It shows that “capability preserved” does not imply “behavioral distribution preserved.” That distinction matters for post-training edits, alignment surgery, and any low-rank or directional intervention that is assumed to be localized. If a refusal direction overlaps with broader traits such as caution, hesitation, or conservatism, then editing it can alter downstream agent behavior far away from the original target behavior. The paper is therefore a useful cautionary result for anyone treating representation-level edits as modular controls.","technical_approach":"$13","experiments":"$14","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and contribution list to see the exact claim being challenged: not that refusal removal works, but that it is behaviorally surgical. We introduce MADA-RL, a post-training framework that specializes compact models into generator and critic roles and trains them with a debate-aware learning signal, fine-tuning only a small subset of parameters via LoRA adapters. Our central contribution is a counterfactual c","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-20T14:38:00Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2607_18006.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.MA"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是一个后训练问题：在 1.5B 这类紧凑模型上，如何用较低训练成本把多智能体 debate 真的变成可学习的推理增益，而不是只是在测试时多采样、多轮讨论。作者认为关键瓶颈不在 debate 协议本身，而在 credit assignment——尤其是 critic 角色到底该因为什么被奖励。如果 critic 只是重复正确答案，它学不到“纠错”能力；如果奖励设计太粗，又容易把 debate 退化成冗余生成。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是直接贡献，更接近 post-train / RL for reasoning 的轻量化方案，所以相关性中等。但有两个启发点值得保留。第一，它把 role specialization 做得比较明确：generator 负责提出候选，critic 负责基于他人答案修正，这种功能拆分和预训练里常见的统一参数范式不同，有助于思考后训练是否需要更结构化的行为分工。第二，它提出的 counterfactual critic advantage，本质是在没有 value model、没有外部 verifier 的情况下，用生成器集体表现构造动态 baseline，这对小模型 RL 稳定性和奖励信号设计有一定参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法由两部分组成。第一部分是一个刻意保持简单的 test-time debate 协议：多 generator 并行给出初始答案，随后 critic 在多轮中顺序读取其他 agent 的输出并进行修正。作者强调这不是复杂的对抗式辩论，也不依赖 judge，而是低成本 deliberation。\n\n第二部分才是论文真正的核心：MADA-RL 的训练信号。作者基于 GRPO 和 LoRA 做参数高效微调，把模型分成 generator 和 critic 两类角色，并为 critic 设计 counterfactual advantage。直观上，critic 的 advantage 不是“这题答对了就奖励”，而是“你的结果相对 generator ensemble 的实例级准确率提升了多少”。也就是用生成器群体在该样本上的表现当动态、角色条件化的 baseline。这样做的目的，是让 critic 学会在 generator 已经大概率正确时少做无效改写，在 generator 容易错的样本上提供真正的纠偏。\n\n和常见 RLHF / outcome reward 方法相比，这个设计少了 value head、replay buffer、外部 verifier 等额外组件，工程上更轻。和单模型 self-refine 相比，它把“提出答案”和“批改答案”分成不同适配器角色，避免一个 LoRA 同时兼顾两种行为。论文还强调只微调少量参数，因此训练成本相对可控。\n\n不过方法边界也很清楚。它没有提出新的 debate 架构，debate 协议本身较朴素；真正的新意主要在 advantage 设计和 role-specialized parameter-efficient RL。若离开数学推理这类可自动判分任务，奖励构造是否仍然稳健，要打问号。","experiments":"实验基座是 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B。作者在其上训练一组 LoRA agent，并且还把若干社区已有 1.5B 变体放到统一协议下比较。评测重点是标准数学推理 benchmark，在 single-agent 和 multi-agent debate 两种设置下报告结果，并分析训练参数量与推理 token 开销的取舍。\n\n从引言可确认的主结论有三点。第一，MADA-RL 在紧凑模型上能带来推理提升，而且提升并不只是因为测试时多轮 deliberation；作者做了控制实验，试图把收益归因到 critic 学到的 corrective behavior。第二，counterfactual critic advantage 比更朴素的奖励定义更能稳定 credit assignment。第三，LoRA 级别的参数更新就足以让 1.5B 模型形成一定的角色分工。\n\n但就全文信息来看，读者需要特别留意具体数字是否扎实：包括每个角色的 LoRA rank、可训练参数占比、debate 轮数、每题额外生成 token、以及在各数学 benchmark 上的绝对提升幅度。如果这些提升主要来自更高测试时 compute，而不是训练后 critic 质量提升，那么方法价值会被高估。作者声称做了这类剥离分析，这部分是实验可信度的关键。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看第 2 节 test-time debate，把协议读清楚，避免把它误解成复杂多智能体系统。然后直接看第 3 节 advantage 设计，尤其是 counterfactual baseline 的定义和它为什么只给 critic 用。这里是整篇最有信息量的部分。\n\n实验部分建议重点看三类内容：一是与普通 single-agent RL 或无 debate 微调的对比；二是 ablation，确认收益来自 role specialization 还是单纯来自更多采样和更多 token；三是成本分析，尤其是训练参数量和推理 token form-","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-18T08:02:50Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_16693.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答两个更细的机制问题。第一，LLM attention pattern 或整体表征相似度，因此结论更接近可干预的机制解释。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接启发不在于提出新训练法，而在于它给了一个较清楚的失败归因框架：算术能力里至少有一部分是格式不变的、可复用的中层机制，而跨格式失败更多发生在这些机制是否被正确触发，而不是模型根本没有共享的算术电路。这个判断会影响后续两类工作。其一是数据与 curriculum 设计：如果共享电路已经存在，增加多格式数据的价值可能主要在于改善触发与路由，而不是重新学一套算术能力。其二是 post-train 或 probing：如果错误主要是激活不足，那么 prompt、格式标准化、或中间表示对齐可能比扩大模型更有效。对 mechanistic interpretability 社区，这篇文章也把“bag of heuristics”从单一符号算术扩展到了 text/code 两种更接近真实使用场景的输入形式。","technical_approach":"方法上分三步。第一步，作者在 Llama-3-8B、Llama-3.2-3B、Llama-3.2-1B 上构造三种等价算术输入：symbolic、word problem、Python code，并刻意选择 tokenizer 能把 0 到 999 表成单 token 的模型，避免多 token 数字把 neuron attribution 搅混。第二步，用两阶段神经元发现流程定位算术启发式神经元：先做 attribution patching 找到对正确答案有高贡献的候选层和候选神经元，再做 activation patching 验证这些神经元是否具有因果作用，而不是仅仅相关。第三步，比较三种格式下识别出的神经元集合重叠度，并做跨格式干预：在一种格式中找到的神经元，拿去消融或 patch 到另一种格式，观察性能变化。\n\n文章的关键点不是简单算 Jaccard overlap，而是把“共享神经元”与“共享功能”分开看。作者发现一小批紧凑的神经元在三种格式中都反复出现，并且这些神经元对应 Nikankin 等人提出的 range、modulo、pattern、identical operands、multi-result 等启发式类别。也就是说，模型内部确实存在跨格式复用的算术启发式库。与此同时，不同格式会改变这些神经元的激活强度、触发频率和组合方式，尤其自然语言和代码更依赖前面的抽象与 operand routing 是否把问题整理成这些神经元能消费的内部表示。作者据此提出：跨格式失败更像 shared circuit under different states，而不是 format-specific circuits。\n\n和已有工作相比，区别主要有三点。第一，之前的算术机制分析大多停留在 canonical symbolic arithmetic；这篇把问题推进到形式不变性。第二，之前很多工作证明“有算术神经元”，这篇进一步问“这些神经元是否跨输入表面形式稳定”。第三，它把 failure analysis 从行为层面的 accuracy drop，推进到 neuron-level causal analysis，因此能区分“没有能力”和“能力未被调用”这两种失败来源。","experiments":"主实验覆盖三种 Llama-3 base 模型，正文重点展示 8B，附录给出 3B 和 1B，结论趋势一致。核心结果有四类。\n\n第一，跨格式共享性。作者识别到一组数量不大的神经元，在 symbolic、text、code 三种输入中都具有稳定的高归因和因果作用；这些共享神经元主要位于中后层，与先前算术神经元分布结论一致。虽然摘要截断了具体比例，但正文结论很明确：共享集合不是边缘现象，而是足以解释相当一部分算术行为的紧凑子集。\n\n第二，消融实验。消融这些共享神经元后，三种格式的算术性能都会明显下降，而非算术能力相对保留。这说明它们不是某种格式特有的 lexical neuron，而是更接近真正的 arithmetic heuristic neurons。更重要的是，用某一格式中发现的神经元去干预另一格式，也能造成显著影响，支持跨格式因果可迁移性。\n\n第三，激活态分析。作者比较了同一批神经元在不同格式下的 activation pattern，发现失败样本往往不是完全缺失相关神经元，而是这些神经元激活不足、激活时机不对，或与其他启发式组合失衡。这个结果直接支持“shared circuit, different activation states”的解释。\n\n第四，尺度一致性。1B、3B、8B 都观察到相同定性趋势，说明这种形式不变性不是只在大模型里偶然出现，也不是某个 checkpoint 的特例。对机制研究来说，这一点很重要，因为它暗示共享算术启发式可能是训练中较早形成、并随规模延续的结构。\n\n如果只关心可操作结论，最值得记的是：模型跨格式掉点，并不自动意味着它没有抽象出统一算术机制；更常见的情况是统一机制存在，但上游表征整理和触发条件不稳。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不必逐行啃。建议先看 Introduction 里的两个研究问题，再看 Experimental Setup，确认三种输入格式如何构造、为什么要选单 token 数字范围。随后重点看神经元发现流程：attribution patching 与 activation patching 各自解决什么问题，为什么两者要结合。只看 attribution 容易把相关性当因果，这里是方法上最该留意的地方。\n\n结果部分优先看三类图表：一是不同格式识别出的神经元重叠与层分布；二是跨格式消融/patching 的性能变化；三是共享神经元在成功与失败样本上的激活差异。如果时间有限，读这些就够了。预计 30 到 45 分钟能抓住主结论。\n\n值得深挖的点有两个。第一，作者把 text/code 失败解释为触发问题，这和 pretrain 里的 data mixture、format balancing、以及 tokenizer 设计能否改善 routing 很相关。第二，文章聚焦 MLP neurons，但没有把 attention routing 机制完全展开；如果要继续研究，最自然的下一步是把“谁把 operand 送到这些神经元面前”补齐，形成从输入格式到最终算术输出的完整电路图。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks two mechanistic questions about arithmetic in LLMs. First, are arithmetic heuristic neurons form-invariant, meaning that the same underlying neurons are recruited when an equivalent computation is written as a symbolic expression, a natural-language word problem, or Python code? Second, when performance differs across formats, is the failure caused by different circuits being used, or by different activation states of a shared circuit? This matters because prior work mostly documented behavioral sensitivity to phrasing, but did not localize whether the bottleneck lies in abstraction, routing, or the final arithmetic machinery itself.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is diagnostic rather than algorithmic. The main takeaway is that a meaningful part of arithmetic competence appears to live in reusable, format-invariant neuron-level machinery, while cross-format failures are often better explained by mis-triggering or weak activation than by the absence of a shared arithmetic circuit. That has implications for data design and training strategy. If the core circuit is already shared, adding multi-format data may help more by improving routing and activation conditions than by teaching arithmetic from scratch again. It also matters for post-training and prompting: normalization, format conversion, or intermediate representation shaping may recover capability more efficiently than scaling the model. For mechanistic interpretability, the paper extends the “bag of heuristics” view beyond canonical symbolic arithmetic into text and code.","technical_approach":"$17","experiments":"$18","reading_guide":"$19","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇不一定改训练 recipe，但会影响你对“模型学到的是模板还是抽象算法”的判断；先看共享神经元识别和跨格式激活迁移实验是否干净。","en":"This may not change your training recipe, but it does affect whether you think the model learned templates or abstract algorithms; first check how clean the shared-neuron identification and cross-format activation transfer really are."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Indian Institute of Technology Bombay","Jahangirnagar University","National Institute of Technology Silchar","Indian Institute of Technology Bombay, Mumbai","Jahangirnagar University, Dhaka, Bangladesh","National Institute of Technology, Silchar"],"authors":["Sharath Naganna","Tanvir Ahmed Sijan","Uddipta Kalita"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["mechanistic-interpretability","arithmetic","neurons","llama-3","format-invariance"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16693","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.16693","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.18228","title":"Logical Judgments Under Pressure: Diagnosing Syllogistic Stability with Learned Soft Prefixes","abstract":"To test how correct logical judgments respond to learned context, we prepend a soft prefix to an exactly labeled syllogistic reasoning benchmark while keeping the model fixed. It asks whether a logically correct judgment remains stable once a learned contextual perturbation is added. The setup is deliberately strict: the model is frozen, labels are exact, and a short soft prefix is trained to flip judgments such as unsatisfiable→satisfiable or valid→invalid. Those reversals are not licensed by the benchmark semantics, so a successful prefix cannot be interpreted as merely adding a legitimate premise. The real question is what kind of mechanism causes the flip: forcing a fixed output symbol, inducing a broad answer-level bias within a task, or approximating a more general logical operation.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is diagnostic rather than algorithmic.
